{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1780501987177,"sessionID":"ses_171d1becaffeH4XpTssqiwjTli","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_KBT5RT310lYtnQ0ykGFT4gmt","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/app/agent/pkg/docker/pull_log_formatter.go","limit":300},"output":"<path>/app/agent/pkg/docker/pull_log_formatter.go</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: package docker\n2: \n3: import (\n4: \t\"fmt\"\n5: \t\"time\"\n6: \n7: \t\"github.com/docker/docker/pkg/jsonmessage\"\n8: \t\"github.com/sirupsen/logrus\"\n9: \n10: \t\"github.com/determined-ai/determined/master/pkg/ptrs\"\n11: )\n12: \n13: type pullInfo struct {\n14: \tDownloadStarted bool\n15: \tExtractStarted  bool\n16: \tTotal           int64\n17: \tDownloaded      int64\n18: \tExtracted       int64\n19: }\n20: \n21: type pullLogFormatter struct {\n22: \tOrder   []string\n23: \tKnown   map[string]*pullInfo\n24: \tBackoff time.Time\n25: }\n26: \n27: // renderProgress generates human-readable and log-file-friendly progress messages.\n28: //\n29: // Every layer goes through the following stages:\n30: // - 1 Pulling fs layer (ID but no size)\n31: // - 1 Waiting (ID but no size)\n32: // - 1+ Downloading\n33: // - 1 Verifying Checksum\n34: // - 1 Download Complete\n35: // - 1+ Extracting\n36: // - 1 Pull Complete\n37: //\n38: // You can't really estimate global progress because the log stream doesn't tell you how big the\n39: // full download size is at any point, it only tells you how big each layer is, and only when that\n40: // layer starts downloading.  The downloads are staggered, so when many layers are present you\n41: // wouldn't know the full download size until you're basically done.\n42: //\n43: // Showing a per-layer status bar is practically impossible without an interactive terminal (as\n44: // docker run would have).\n45: //\n46: // So instead we create a weighted-average status bar, where every layer's download and extraction\n47: // count as equal parts.  The status bar ends up pretty jerky but it still gives a \"sensation\" of\n48: // progress; things don't look frozen, the user has a rough idea of how far along you are, and the\n49: // logs are still sane afterwards.\n50: func (f *pullLogFormatter) RenderProgress() string {\n51: \tvar downloaded int64\n52: \tvar extracted int64\n53: \tprogress := 0.0\n54: \tfor _, id := range f.Order {\n55: \t\tinfo := f.Known[id]\n56: \t\tdownloaded += info.Downloaded\n57: \t\textracted += info.Extracted\n58: \t\tswitch {\n59: \t\tcase !info.DownloadStarted:\n60: \t\t\t// No progress on this layer.\n61: \t\tcase info.Extracted == info.Total:\n62: \t\t\t// this layer is complete\n63: \t\t\tprogress += 1.0\n64: \t\tcase info.Downloaded == info.Total:\n65: \t\t\t// download complete, extraction in progress\n66: \t\t\tprogress += 0.5 + 0.5*float64(info.Extracted)/float64(info.Total)\n67: \t\tdefault:\n68: \t\t\tprogress += 0.5 * float64(info.Downloaded) / float64(info.Total)\n69: \t\t}\n70: \t}\n71: \n72: \t// Normalize by layer count.\n73: \tprogress /= float64(len(f.Known))\n74: \n75: \t// 40-character progress bar.\n76: \tprog := int(40.0 * progress)\n77: \n78: \tbar := \"\"\n79: \tfor i := 0; i < 40; i++ {\n80: \t\tif i <= prog {\n81: \t\t\tif prog == 40 || i+1 <= prog {\n82: \t\t\t\t// Download is full, or middle of bar.\n83: \t\t\t\tbar += \"=\"\n84: \t\t\t} else {\n85: \t\t\t\t// Boundary between bar and spaces.\n86: \t\t\t\tbar += \">\"\n87: \t\t\t}\n88: \t\t} else {\n89: \t\t\tbar += \" \"\n90: \t\t}\n91: \t}\n92: \n93: \treturn fmt.Sprintf(\n94: \t\t\"[%v] Downloaded: %.1fMB, Extracted %.1fMB\",\n95: \t\tbar,\n96: \t\tfloat64(downloaded)/1e6,\n97: \t\tfloat64(extracted)/1e6,\n98: \t)\n99: }\n100: \n101: func (f *pullLogFormatter) backoffOrRenderProgress() *string {\n102: \t// Log at most one line every 1 second.\n103: \tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n104: \tif now.Before(f.Backoff) {\n105: \t\treturn nil\n106: \t}\n107: \tf.Backoff = now.Add(1 * time.Second)\n108: \n109: \treturn ptrs.Ptr(f.RenderProgress())\n110: }\n111: \n112: // Update returns nil or a rendered progress update for the end user.\n113: func (f *pullLogFormatter) Update(msg jsonmessage.JSONMessage) *string {\n114: \tif msg.Error != nil {\n115: \t\tlogrus.Errorf(\"%d: %v\", msg.Error.Code, msg.Error.Message)\n116: \t\treturn nil\n117: \t}\n118: \n119: \tvar info *pullInfo\n120: \tvar ok bool\n121: \n122: \tswitch msg.Status {\n123: \tcase \"Pulling fs layer\", \"Waiting\":\n124: \t\tif _, ok = f.Known[msg.ID]; !ok {\n125: \t\t\t// New layer!\n126: \t\t\tf.Known[msg.ID] = &pullInfo{}\n127: \t\t\tf.Order = append(f.Order, msg.ID)\n128: \t\t}\n129: \t\treturn nil\n130: \n131: \tcase \"Downloading\":\n132: \t\tif info, ok = f.Known[msg.ID]; !ok {\n133: \t\t\tlogrus.Error(\"message ID not found for downloading message!\")\n134: \t\t\treturn nil\n135: \t\t}\n136: \t\tif info.ExtractStarted {\n137: \t\t\tlogrus.Error(\"got downloading message after extraction started!\")\n138: \t\t\treturn nil\n139: \t\t}\n140: \t\tinfo.Downloaded = msg.Progress.Current\n141: \t\t// The first \"Downloading\" msg is important, as it gives us the layer size.\n142: \t\tif !info.DownloadStarted {\n143: \t\t\tinfo.DownloadStarted = true\n144: \t\t\tinfo.Total = msg.Progress.Total\n145: \t\t}\n146: \t\treturn f.backoffOrRenderProgress()\n147: \n148: \tcase \"Extracting\":\n149: \t\tif info, ok = f.Known[msg.ID];
